OSWEGO RIVER AT LOCK 7, OSWEGO NY, USGS site 04249000 (USGS-04249000), stream in Oswego County, New York, draining 5100 square miles. Daily means from 1900-10-01 to 2026-10-12, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
